\"    def prepare_images(self, batch):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Apply SmolVLA preprocessing to the images, like resizing to 224x224 and padding to keep aspect ratio, and\\n        convert pixel range from [0.0, 1.0] to [-1.0, 1.0] as requested by SigLIP.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        images = []\\n        img_masks = []\\n        present_img_keys = [key for key in self.config.image_features if key in batch]\\n        missing_img_keys = [key for key in self.config.image_features if key not in batch]\\n\\n        if len(present_img_keys) == 0:\\n            raise ValueError(\\n                f\\\"All image features are missing from the batch. At least one expected. (batch: {batch.keys()}) (image_features:{self.config.image_features})\\\"\\n            )\\n        # Preprocess image features present in the batch\\n        for key in present_img_keys:\\n            img = batch[key][:, -1, :, :, :] if batch[key].ndim == 5 else batch[key]\\n            if self.config.resize_imgs_with_padding is not None:\\n                img = resize_with_pad(img, *self.config.resize_imgs_with_padding, pad_value=0)\\n\\n            # Normalize from range [0,1] to [-1,1] as expacted by siglip\\n            img = img * 2.0 - 1.0\\n\\n            bsize = img.shape[0]\\n            device = img.device\\n            if f\\\"{key}_padding_mask\\\" in batch:\\n                mask = batch[f\\\"{key}_padding_mask\\\"].bool()\\n            else:\\n                mask = torch.ones(bsize, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)\\n            images.append(img)\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\n\\n        # Create image features not present in the batch\\n        # as fully 0 padded images.\\n        for num_empty_cameras in range(len(missing_img_keys)):\\n            if num_empty_cameras >= self.config.empty_cameras:\\n                break\\n            img = torch.ones_like(img) * -1\\n            mask = torch.zeros_like(mask)\\n            images.append(img)\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\n        return images, img_masks\\n\\n    def _pi_aloha_decode_state(self, state):\\n        # Flip the joints.\\n        for motor_idx in [1, 2, 8, 9]:\\n            state[:, motor_idx] *= -1\\n        # Reverse the gripper transformation that is being applied by the Aloha runtime.\\n        for motor_idx in [6, 13]:\\n            state[:, motor_idx] = aloha_gripper_to_angular(state[:, motor_idx])\\n        return state\\n\\n    def _pi_aloha_encode_actions(self, actions):\\n        # Flip the joints.\\n        for motor_idx in [1, 2, 8, 9]:\\n            actions[:, :, motor_idx] *= -1\\n        # Reverse the gripper transformation that is being applied by the Aloha runtime.\\n        for motor_idx in [6, 13]:\\n            actions[:, :, motor_idx] = aloha_gripper_from_angular(actions[:, :, motor_idx])\\n        return actions\\n\\n    def _pi_aloha_encode_actions_inv(self, actions):\\n        # Flip the joints again.\\n        for motor_idx in [1, 2, 8, 9]:\\n            actions[:, :, motor_idx] *= -1\\n        # Reverse the gripper transformation that is being applied by the Aloha runtime.\\n        for motor_idx in [6, 13]:\\n            actions[:, :, motor_idx] = aloha_gripper_from_angular_inv(actions[:, :, motor_idx])\\n        return actions\\n\\n    def prepare_state(self, batch):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Pad state\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        state = batch[OBS_STATE][:, -1, :] if batch[OBS_STATE].ndim > 2 else batch[OBS_STATE]\\n        state = pad_vector(state, self.config.max_state_dim)\\n        return state\\n\\n    def prepare_action(self, batch):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Pad action\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        actions = pad_vector(batch[ACTION], self.config.max_action_dim)\\n        return actions\\n\\n    def _get_default_peft_targets(self) -> dict[str, any]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return default PEFT target modules for SmolVLA fine-tuning.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        common_projections = (\\n            \\\"state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out\\\"\\n        )\\n        target_modules = rf\\\"(model\\\\.vlm_with_expert\\\\.lm_expert\\\\..*\\\\.(q|v)_proj|model\\\\.({common_projections}))\\\"\\n        return {\\n            \\\"target_modules\\\": target_modules,\\n            \\\"modules_to_save\\\": [],\\n        }\\n\\n    def _validat\\n\\n... Apart from the loss which\\\\n                is a Tensor, all other items should be logging-friendly, native Python types.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        raise NotImplementedError\\\\n\\\\n    @abc.abstractmethod\\\\n    def predict_action_chunk(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], **kwargs: Unpack[ActionSelectKwargs]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Returns the action chunk (for action chunking policies) for a given observation, potentially in batch mode.\\\\n\\\\n        Child classes using action chunking should use this method within `select_action` to form the action chunk\\\\n        cached for selection.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        raise NotImplementedError\\\\n\\\\n    @abc.abstractmethod\\\\n    def select_action(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], **kwargs: Unpack[ActionSelectKwargs]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return one action to run in the environment (potentially in batch mode).\\\\n\\\\n        When the model uses a history of observations, or outputs a sequence of actions, this method deals\\\\n        with caching.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        raise NotImplementedError\\\\n\\\\n    def push_model_to_hub(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        cfg: TrainPipelineConfig,\\\\n        peft_model=None,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        api = HfApi()\\\\n        repo_id = api.create_repo(\\\\n            repo_id=self.config.repo_id, private=self.config.private, exist_ok=True\\\\n        ).repo_id\\\\n\\\\n        # Push the files to the repo in a single commit\\\\n        with TemporaryDirectory(ignore_cleanup_errors=True) as tmp:\\\\n            saved_path = Path(tmp) / repo_id\\\\n\\\\n            if peft_model is not None:\\\\n                # Since PEFT just forwards calls to `push_model_to_hub`, `self` is not the PeftModel wrapper\\\\n                # but the actual policy which is why we need the PEFT model passed to us to save the adapter.\\\\n                # That also means that we need to store the policy config ourselves since PEFT can't.\\\\n                peft_model.save_pretrained(saved_path)\\\\n                self.config.save_pretrained(saved_path)\\\\n            else:\\\\n                self.save_pretrained(saved_path)  # Calls _save_pretrained and stores model tensors\\\\n\\\\n            card = self.generate_model_card(\\\\n                cfg.dataset.repo_id, self.config.type, self.config.license, self.config.tags\\\\n            )\\\\n            card.save(str(saved_path / \\\\\\\"README.md\\\\\\\"))\\\\n\\\\n            cfg.save_pretrained(saved_path)  # Calls _save_pretrained and stores train config\\\\n\\\\n            commit_info = api.upload_folder(\\\\n                repo_id=repo_id,\\\\n                repo_type=\\\\\\\"model\\\\\\\",\\\\n                folder_path=saved_path,\\\\n                commit_message=\\\\\\\"Upload policy weights, train config and readme\\\\\\\",\\\\n                allow_patterns=[\\\\\\\"*.safetensors\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"*.json\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"*.yaml\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"*.md\\\\\\\"],\\\\n                ignore_patterns=[\\\\\\\"*.tmp\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"*.log\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n            logging.info(f\\\\\\\"Model pushed to {commit_info.repo_url.url}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    def generate_model_card(\\\\n        self, dataset_repo_id: str, model_type: str, license: str | None, tags: list[str] | None\\\\n    ) -> ModelCard:\\\\n        base_model = \\\\\\\"lerobot/smolvla_base\\\\\\\" if model_type == \\\\\\\"smolvla\\\\\\\" else None  # Set a base model\\\\n\\\\n        card_data = ModelCardData(\\\\n            license=license or \\\\\\\"apache-2.0\\\\\\\",\\\\n            library_name=\\\\\\\"lerobot\\\\\\\",\\\\n            pipeline_tag=\\\\\\\"robotics\\\\\\\",\\\\n            tags=list(set(tags or []).union({\\\\\\\"robotics\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"lerobot\\\\\\\", model_type})),\\\\n            model_name=model_type,\\\\n            datasets=dataset_repo_id,\\\\n            base_model=base_model,\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        template_card = (\\\\n            files(\\\\\\\"lerobot.templates\\\\\\\").joinpath(\\\\\\\"lerobot_modelcard_template.md\\\\\\\").read_text(encoding=\\\\\\\"utf-8\\\\\\\")\\\\n        )\\\\n        card = ModelCard.from_template(card_data, template_str=template_card)\\\\n        card.validate()\\\\n        return card\\\\n\\\\n    def wrap_with_peft(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        peft_config=None,\\\\n        peft_cli_overrides: dict | None = None,\\\\n    ) -> \\\\\\\"PreTrainedPolicy\\\\\\\":\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Wrap this policy w\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 2524 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\n validate.\\\\n\\\\n        Raises:\\\\n            ValueError: If the configuration is invalid.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if not self.config.pretrained_path:\\\\n            raise ValueError(\\\\n                \\\\\\\"Training from scratch using PEFT is unlikely to yield good results. \\\\\\\"\\\\n                \\\\\\\"Supply a `policy.pretrained_path` to fine-tune an existing model.\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    def _preprocess_peft_cli_overrides(self, cli_overrides: dict, peft_method_type) -> dict:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Preprocess CLI overrides: rename keys and handle method-specific init_type.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            cli_overrides: Dict of CLI options (will be copied, not mutated).\\\\n            peft_method_type: The PeftType enum value for the PEFT method.\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            Preprocessed dict with renamed keys and init_type mapped to method-specific key.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        from peft import PeftType\\\\n\\\\n        cli_overrides = cli_overrides.copy()\\\\n\\\\n        # Handle the full_training_modules -> modules_to_save rename\\\\n        if \\\\\\\"full_training_modules\\\\\\\" in cli_overrides:\\\\n            cli_overrides[\\\\\\\"modules_to_save\\\\\\\"] = cli_overrides.pop(\\\\\\\"full_training_modules\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        # Remove method_type as it's handled separately\\\\n        cli_overrides.pop(\\\\\\\"method_type\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n\\\\n        # Handle init_type specially based on PEFT method\\\\n        init_type = cli_overrides.pop(\\\\\\\"init_type\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n        if init_type is not None:\\\\n            if peft_method_type == PeftType.LORA:\\\\n                cli_overrides[\\\\\\\"init_lora_weights\\\\\\\"] = init_type\\\\n            elif peft_method_type == PeftType.MISS:\\\\n                cli_overrides[\\\\\\\"init_weights\\\\\\\"] = init_type\\\\n            else:\\\\n                raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\"Init type '{init_type}' unknown for PEFT method {peft_method_type}.\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        return cli_overrides\\\\n\\\\n    def _build_peft_config(self, cli_overrides: dict):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Build a PEFT config from policy defaults and CLI overrides.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        from peft import PEFT_TYPE_TO_CONFIG_MAPPING, PeftType\\\\n\\\\n        # Determine PEFT method type (default to LORA)\\\\n        method_type_str = cli_overrides.get(\\\\\\\"method_type\\\\\\\") or \\\\\\\"lora\\\\\\\"\\\\n        peft_method_type = PeftType[method_type_str.upper()]\\\\n        peft_config_cls = PEFT_TYPE_TO_CONFIG_MAPPING[peft_method_type]\\\\n\\\\n        # Preprocess CLI overrides\\\\n        cli_overrides = self._preprocess_peft_cli_overrides(cli_overrides, peft_method_type)\\\\n\\\\n        # Start with policy defaults, apply CLI overrides\\\\n        config_dict = dict(self._get_default_peft_targets() or {})\\\\n        for key, value in cli_overrides.items():\\\\n            if value is not None:\\\\n                config_dict[key] = value\\\\n\\\\n        # All rights reserved.\\\\n#\\\\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \\\\\\\"License\\\\\\\");\\\\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\\\\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\\\\n#\\\\n#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\\\\n#\\\\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\\\\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \\\\\\\"AS IS\\\\\\\" BASIS,\\\\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\\\\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\\\\n# limitations under the License.\\\\n\\\\nfrom typing import Any\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\n\\\\nfrom lerobot.processor import (\\\\n    AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep,\\\\n    DeviceProcessorStep,\\\\n    NewLineTaskProcessorStep,\\\\n    NormalizerProcessorStep,\\\\n    PolicyAction,\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline,\\\\n    RenameObservationsProcessorStep,\\\\n    TokenizerProcessorStep,\\\\n    UnnormalizerProcessorStep,\\\\n    policy_action_to_transition,\\\\n    transition_to_policy_action,\\\\n)\\\\nfrom lerobot.utils.constants import POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME, POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME\\\\n\\\\nfrom .configuration_smolvla import SmolVLAConfig\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_smolvla_pre_post_processors(\\\\n    config: SmolVLAConfig,\\\\n    dataset_stats: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,\\\\n) -> tuple[\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]],\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction],\\\\n]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Constructs pre-processor and post-processor pipelines for the SmolVLA policy.\\\\n\\\\n    The pre-processing pipeline prepares input data for the model by:\\\\n    1.  Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\\\n    2.  Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\\\n    3.  Adding a batch dimension.\\\\n    4.  Ensuring the language task description ends with a newline character.\\\\n    5.  Tokenizing the language task description.\\\\n    6.  Moving all data to the specified device.\\\\n\\\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\\\n    1.  Moving data to the CPU.\\\\n    2.  Unnormalizing the output actions to their original scale.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        config: The configuration object for the SmolVLA policy.\\\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    input_steps = [\\\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\\\n        NewLineTaskProcessorStep(),\\\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            tokenizer_name=config.vlm_model_name,\\\\n            padding=config.pad_language_to,\\\\n            padding_side=\\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\",\\\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    ]\\\\n    output_steps = [\\\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\"),\\\\n    ]\\\\n    return (\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\\\n            steps=input_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\\\n            steps=output_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.35}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2e5005b7-4383-4494-8f3b-f04ce9f94618","uuid":"b4cb2f13-03d1-4ed2-85d6-1d5721ee7ff6","timestamp":"2026-05-22T17:04:19.981Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python\\\\n\\\\n# Copyright 2025 HuggingFace Inc. team. Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\\\n    2.  Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\\\n    3.  Adding a batch dimension.\\\\n    4.  Ensuring the language task description ends with a newline character.\\\\n    5.  Tokenizing the language task description.\\\\n    6. Unnormalizing the output actions to their original scale.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        config: The configuration object for the SmolVLA policy.\\\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    input_steps = [\\\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # All rights reserved.\\n#\\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \\\"License\\\");\\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\\n#\\n#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\\n#\\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \\\"AS IS\\\" BASIS,\\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\\n# limitations under the License.\\n\\nfrom typing import Any\\n\\nimport torch\\n\\nfrom lerobot.processor import (\\n    AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep,\\n    DeviceProcessorStep,\\n    NewLineTaskProcessorStep,\\n    NormalizerProcessorStep,\\n    PolicyAction,\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline,\\n    RenameObservationsProcessorStep,\\n    TokenizerProcessorStep,\\n    UnnormalizerProcessorStep,\\n    policy_action_to_transition,\\n    transition_to_policy_action,\\n)\\nfrom lerobot.utils.constants import POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME, POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME\\n\\nfrom .configuration_smolvla import SmolVLAConfig\\n\\n\\ndef make_smolvla_pre_post_processors(\\n    config: SmolVLAConfig,\\n    dataset_stats: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,\\n) -> tuple[\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]],\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction],\\n]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Constructs pre-processor and post-processor pipelines for the SmolVLA policy.\\n\\n    The pre-processing pipeline prepares input data for the model by:\\n    1.  Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\n    2.  Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\n    3.  Adding a batch dimension.\\n    4.  Ensuring the language task description ends with a newline character.\\n    5.  Tokenizing the language task description.\\n    6.  Moving all data to the specified device.\\n\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\n    1.  Moving data to the CPU.\\n    2.  Unnormalizing the output actions to their original scale.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        config: The configuration object for the SmolVLA policy.\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    input_steps = [\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\n        NewLineTaskProcessorStep(),\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\n            tokenizer_name=config.vlm_model_name,\\n            padding=config.pad_language_to,\\n            padding_side=\\\"right\\\",\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\n        ),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\n        ),\\n    ]\\n    output_steps = [\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\n        ),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\"cpu\\\"),\\n    ]\\n    return (\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\n            steps=input_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n        ),\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\n            steps=output_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\n        ),\\n    )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}}}

License.\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\n\\nimport einops\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import Tensor\\n\\nfrom lerobot.configs import PipelineFeatureType, PolicyFeature\\nfrom lerobot.utils.constants import OBS_ENV_STATE, OBS_IMAGE, OBS_IMAGES, OBS_STATE, OBS_STR\\n\\nfrom .pipeline import ObservationProcessorStep, ProcessorStepRegistry\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\n@ProcessorStepRegistry.register(name=\\\"observation_processor\\\")\\nclass VanillaObservationProcessorStep(ObservationProcessorStep):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Processes standard Gymnasium observations into the LeRobot format.\\n\\n    This step handles both image and state data from a typical observation dictionary,\\n    preparing it for use in a LeRobot policy.\\n\\n    **Image Processing:**\\n    -   Converts channel-last (H, W, C), `uint8` images to channel-first (C, H, W),\\n        `float32` tensors.\\n    -   Normalizes pixel values from the [0, 255] range to [0, 1].\\n    -   Adds a batch dimension if one is not already present.\\n    -   Recognizes a single image under the key `\\\"pixels\\\"` and maps it to\\n        `\\\"observation.image\\\"`.\\n    -   Recognizes a dictionary of images under the key `\\\"pixels\\\"` and maps them\\n        to `\\\"observation.images.{camera_name}\\\"`.\\n\\n    **State Processing:**\\n    -   Maps the `\\\"environment_state\\\"` key to `\\\"observation.environment_state\\\"`.\\n    -   Maps the `\\\"agent_pos\\\"` key to `\\\"observation.state\\\"`.\\n    -   Converts NumPy arrays to PyTorch tensors.\\n    -   Adds a batch dimension if one is not already present.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def _process_single_image(self, img: np.ndarray) -> Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Processes a single NumPy image array into a channel-first, normalized tensor.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            img: A NumPy array representing the image, expected to be in channel-last\\n                 (H, W, C) format with a `uint8` dtype.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            A `float32` PyTorch tensor in channel-first (B, C, H, W) format, with\\n            pixel values normalized to the [0, 1] range.\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            ValueError: If the input image does not appear to be in channel-last\\n                        format or is not of `uint8` dtype.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Convert to tensor\\n        img_tensor = torch.from_numpy(img)\\n\\n        # Add batch dimension if needed\\n        if img_tensor.ndim == 3:\\n            img_tensor = img_tensor.unsqueeze(0)\\n\\n        # Validate image format\\n        _, h, w, c = img_tensor.shape\\n        if not (c < h and c < w):\\n            raise ValueError(f\\\"Expected channel-last images, but got shape {img_tensor.shape}\\\")\\n\\n        if img_tensor.dtype != torch.uint8:\\n            raise ValueError(f\\\"Expected torch.uint8 images, but got {img_tensor.dtype}\\\")\\n\\n        # Convert to channel-first format\\n        img_tensor = einops.rearrange(img_tensor, \\\"b h w c -> b c h w\\\").contiguous()\\n\\n        # Convert to float32 and normalize to [0, 1]\\n        img_tensor = img_tensor.type(torch.float32) / 255.0\\n\\n        return img_tensor\\n\\n    def _process_observation(self, observation):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Processes both image and state observations.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n        processed_obs = observation.copy()\\n\\n        if \\\"pixels\\\" in processed_obs:\\n            pixels = processed_obs.pop(\\\"pixels\\\")\\n\\n            if isinstance(pixels, dict):\\n                imgs = {f\\\"{OBS_IMAGES}.{key}\\\": img for key, img in pixels.items()}\\n            else:\\n                imgs = {OBS_IMAGE: pixels}\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 63 字符] ...\\n\\nprocessed_obs[imgkey] = self._process_single_image(img)\\n\\n        if \\\"environment_state\\\" in processed_obs:\\n            env_state_np = processed_obs.pop(\\\"environment_state\\\")\\n            env_state = torch.from_numpy(env_state_np).float()\\n            if env_state.dim() == 1:\\n                env_state = env_state.unsqueeze(0)\\n            processed_obs[OBS_ENV_STATE] = env_state\\n\\n        if \\\"agent_pos\\\" in processed_obs:\\n            agent_pos_np = processed_obs.pop(\\\"agent_pos\\\")\\n            agent_pos = torch.from_numpy(agent_pos_np).float()\\n            if agent_pos.dim() == 1:\\n                agent_pos = agent_pos.unsqueeze(0)\\n            processed_obs[OBS_STATE] = agent_pos\\n\\n        return processed_obs\\n\\n    def observation(self, observation):\\n        return self._process_observation(observation)\\n\\n    def transform_features(\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Transforms feature keys from the Gym standard to the LeRobot standard.\\n\\n        This method standardizes the feature dictionary by renaming keys according\\n        to LeRobot's conventions, ensuring that policies can be constructed correctly.\\n        It handles various raw key formats, including those with an \\\"observation.\\\" prefix.\\n\\n        **Renaming Rules:**\\n        - `pixels` or `observation.pixels` -> `observation.image`\\n        - `pixels.{cam}` or `observation.pixels.{cam}` -> `observation.images.{cam}`\\n        - `environment_state` or `observation.environment_state` -> `observation.environment_state`\\n        - `agent_pos` or `observation.agent_pos` -> `observation.state`\\n\\n        Args:\\n            features: The policy features dictionary with Gym-style keys.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            The policy features dictionary with standardized LeRobot keys.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Build a new features mapping keyed by the same FeatureType buckets\\n        # We assume callers already placed features in the correct FeatureType.\\n        new_features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]] = {ft: {} for ft in features}\\n\\n        exact_pairs = {\\n            \\\"pixels\\\": OBS_IMAGE,\\n            \\\"environment_state\\\": OBS_ENV_STATE,\\n            \\\"agent_pos\\\": OBS_STATE,\\n        }\\n\\n        prefix_pairs = {\\n            \\\"pixels.\\\": f\\\"{OBS_IMAGES}.\\\",\\n        }\\n\\n        # Iterate over all incoming feature buckets and normalize/move each entry\\n        for src_ft, bucket in features.items():\\n            for key, feat in list(bucket.items()):\\n                handled = False\\n\\n                # Prefix-based rules (e.g. pixels.cam1 -> OBS_IMAGES.cam1)\\n                for old_prefix, new_prefix in prefix_pairs.items():\\n                    prefixed_old = f\\\"{OBS_STR}.{old_prefix}\\\"\\n                    if key.startswith(prefixed_old):\\n                        suffix = key[len(prefixed_old) :]\\n                        new_key = f\\\"{new_prefix}{suffix}\\\"\\n                        new_features[src_ft][new_key] = feat\\n                        handled = True\\n                        break\\n\\n                    if key.startswith(old_prefix):\\n                        suffix = key[len(old_prefix) :]\\n                        new_key = f\\\"{new_prefix}{suffix}\\\"\\n                        new_features[src_ft][new_key] = feat\\n                        handled = True\\n                        break\\n\\n                if handled:\\n                    continue\\n\\n                # Exact-name rules (pixels, environment_state, agent_pos)\\n                for old, new in exact_pairs.items():\\n                    if key == old or key == f\\\"{OBS_STR}.{old}\\\":\\n                        new_key = new\\n                        new_features[src_ft][new_key] = feat\\n                        handled = True\\n                        break\\n\\n                if handled:\\n                    continue\\n\\n                # Default: keep key in the same source FeatureType bucket\\n                new_features[src_ft][key] = feat\\n\\n        return new_features\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}}}

All rights reserved.\\\\n#\\\\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \\\\\\\"License\\\\\\\");\\\\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\\\\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\\\\n#\\\\n#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\\\\n#\\\\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\\\\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \\\\\\\"AS IS\\\\\\\" BASIS,\\\\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\\\\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\\\\n# limitations under the License.\\\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\\\n\\\\nfrom typing import Any\\\\n\\\\nimport gymnasium as gym\\\\n\\\\nfrom .configs import EnvConfig, HubEnvConfig\\\\nfrom .utils import _call_make_env, _download_hub_file, _import_hub_module, _normalize_hub_result\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_env_config(env_type: str, **kwargs) -> EnvConfig:\\\\n    try:\\\\n        cls = EnvConfig.get_choice_class(env_type)\\\\n    except KeyError as err:\\\\n        raise ValueError(\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Environment type '{env_type}' is not registered. \\\\\\\"\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Available: {list(EnvConfig.get_known_choices().keys())}\\\\\\\"\\\\n        ) from err\\\\n    return cls(**kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_env_pre_post_processors(\\\\n    env_cfg: EnvConfig,\\\\n    policy_cfg: Any,\\\\n) -> tuple[Any, Any]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Create preprocessor and postprocessor pipelines for environment observations.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns a tuple of (preprocessor, postprocessor). By default, delegates to\\\\n    ``env_cfg.get_env_processors()``.  The XVLAConfig policy-specific override\\\\n    stays here because it depends on the *policy* config, not the env config.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    from lerobot.policies.xvla.configuration_xvla import XVLAConfig\\\\n\\\\n    if isinstance(policy_cfg, XVLAConfig):\\\\n        from lerobot.policies.xvla.processor_xvla import make_xvla_libero_pre_post_processors\\\\n\\\\n        return make_xvla_libero_pre_post_processors()\\\\n\\\\n    return env_cfg.get_env_processors()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_env(\\\\n    cfg: EnvConfig | str,\\\\n    n_envs: int = 1,\\\\n    use_async_envs: bool = False,\\\\n    hub_cache_dir: str | None = None,\\\\n    trust_remote_code: bool = False,\\\\n) -> dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Makes a gym vector environment according to the config or Hub reference.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        cfg (EnvConfig | str): Either an `EnvConfig` object describing the environment to build locally,\\\\n            or a Hugging Face Hub repository identifier (e.g. `\\\\\\\"username/repo\\\\\\\"`). In the latter case,\\\\n            the repo must include a Python file (usually `env.py`).\\\\n        n_envs (int, optional): The number of parallelized env to return. Defaults to 1.\\\\n        use_async_envs (bool, optional): Whether to return an AsyncVectorEnv or a SyncVectorEnv. Defaults to\\\\n            False.\\\\n        hub_cache_dir (str | None): Optional cache path for downloaded hub files.\\\\n        trust_remote_code (bool): **Explicit consent** to execute remote code from the Hub.\\\\n            Default False — must be set to True to import/exec hub `env.py`.\\\\n    Raises:\\\\n        ValueError: if n_envs < 1\\\\n        ModuleNotFoundError: If the requested env package is not installed\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\\\n            A mapping from suite name to indexed vectorized environments.\\\\n            - For multi-task benchmarks (e.g., LIBERO): one entry per suite, and one vec env per task_id.\\\\n            - For single-task environments: a single suite entry (cfg.type) with task_id=0.\\\\n\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    # if user passed a hub id string (e.g., \\\\\\\"username/repo\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"username/repo@main:env.py\\\\\\\")\\\\n    # simplified: only support hub-provided `make_env`\\\\n    # TODO: (jadechoghari): deprecate string API and remove this check\\\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\\\n        hub_path: str | None = cfg\\\\n    elif isinstance(cfg, HubEnvConfig):\\\\n        hub_path = cfg.hub_path\\\\n    else:\\\\n        hub_path = None\\\\n\\\\n    # If hub_path is set, download and call hub-provided `make_env`\\\\n    if hub_path:\\\\n        # _download_hub_file will raise the same RuntimeError if trust_remote_code is False\\\\n        repo_id, file_path, local_file, revision = _download_hub_file(\\\\n            hub_path, trust_remote_code, hub_cache_dir\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        # import and surface clear import errors\\\\n        module = _import_hub_module(local_file, repo_id)\\\\n\\\\n        # call the hub-provided make_env\\\\n        env_cfg = None if isinstance(cfg, str) else cfg\\\\n        raw_result = _call_make_env(module, n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs, cfg=env_cfg)\\\\n\\\\n        # normalize the return into {suite: {task_id: vec_env}}\\\\n        return _normalize_hub_result(raw_result)\\\\n\\\\n    # At this point, cfg must be an EnvConfig (not a string) since hub_path would have been set otherwise\\\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\\\n        raise TypeError(\\\\\\\"cfg should be an EnvConfig at this point\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    if n_envs < 1:\\\\n        raise ValueError(\\\\\\\"`n_envs` must be at least 1\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    return cfg.create_envs(n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs)\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.36}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2e5005b7-4383-4494-8f3b-f04ce9f94618","uuid":"7c008c3f-083e-4e5a-ba88-7ddcb8b4fb30","timestamp":"2026-05-22T17:05:47.837Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python\\\\n\\\\n# Copyright 2024 The HuggingFace Inc. team. For multi-task benchmarks (e.g., LIBERO): one entry per suite, and one vec env per task_id.\\n            - For single-task environments: a single suite entry (cfg.type) with task_id=0.\\n\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # if user passed a hub id string (e.g., \\\"username/repo\\\", \\\"username/repo@main:env.py\\\")\\n    # simplified: only support hub-provided `make_env`\\n    # TODO: (jadechoghari): deprecate string API and remove this check\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\n        hub_path: str | None = cfg\\n    elif isinstance(cfg, HubEnvConfig):\\n        hub_path = cfg.hub_path\\n    else:\\n        hub_path = None\\n\\n    # If hub_path is set, download and call hub-provided `make_env`\\n    if hub_path:\\n        # _download_hub_file will raise the same RuntimeError if trust_remote_code is False\\n        repo_id, file_path, local_file, revision = _download_hub_file(\\n            hub_path, trust_remote_code, hub_cache_dir\\n        )\\n\\n        # import and surface clear import errors\\n        module = _import_hub_module(local_file, repo_id)\\n\\n        # call the hub-provided make_env\\n        env_cfg = None if isinstance(cfg, str) else cfg\\n        raw_result = _call_make_env(module, n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs, cfg=env_cfg)\\n\\n        # normalize the return into {suite: {task_id: vec_env}}\\n        return _normalize_hub_result(raw_result)\\n\\n    # At this point, cfg must be an EnvConfig (not a string) since hub_path would have been set otherwise\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\n        raise TypeError(\\\"cfg should be an EnvConfig at this point\\\")\\n\\n    if n_envs < 1:\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"`n_envs` must be at least 1\\\")\\n\\n    return cfg.create_envs(n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}}}

TODO(jadechoghari, imstevenpmwork): refactor this to use features from the environment (no hardcoding)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert environment observation to LeRobot format observation.\\n    Args:\\n        observation: Dictionary of observation batches from a Gym vector environment.\\n    Returns:\\n        Dictionary of observation batches with keys renamed to LeRobot format and values as tensors.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # map to expected inputs for the policy\\n    return_observations = {}\\n    if \\\"pixels\\\" in observations:\\n        if isinstance(observations[\\\"pixels\\\"], dict):\\n            imgs = {f\\\"{OBS_IMAGES}.{key}\\\": img for key, img in observations[\\\"pixels\\\"].items()}\\n        else:\\n            imgs = {OBS_IMAGE: observations[\\\"pixels\\\"]}\\n\\n        for imgkey, img in imgs.items():\\n            # TODO(aliberts, rcadene): use transforms.ToTensor()?\\n            img_tensor = torch.from_numpy(img)\\n\\n            # When preprocessing observations in a non-vectorized environment, we need to add a batch dimension.\\n            # This is the case for human-in-the-loop RL where there is only one environment.\\n            if img_tensor.ndim == 3:\\n                img_tensor = img_tensor.unsqueeze(0)\\n            # sanity check that images are channel last\\n            _, h, w, c = img_tensor.shape\\n            assert c < h and c < w, f\\\"expect channel last images, but instead got {img_tensor.shape=}\\\"\\n\\n            # sanity check that images are uint8\\n            assert img_tensor.dtype == torch.uint8, f\\\"expect torch.uint8, but instead {img_tensor.dtype=}\\\"\\n\\n            # convert to channel first of type float32 in range [0,1]\\n            img_tensor = einops.rearrange(img_tensor, \\\"b h w c -> b c h w\\\").contiguous()\\n            img_tensor = img_tensor.type(torch.float32)\\n            img_tensor /= 255\\n\\n            return_observations[imgkey] = img_tensor\\n\\n    if \\\"environment_state\\\" in observations:\\n        env_state = torch.from_numpy(observations[\\\"environment_state\\\"]).float()\\n        if env_state.dim() == 1:\\n            env_state = env_state.unsqueeze(0)\\n\\n        return_observations[OBS_ENV_STATE] = env_state\\n\\n    if \\\"agent_pos\\\" in observations:\\n        agent_pos = torch.from_numpy(observations[\\\"agent_pos\\\"]).float()\\n        if agent_pos.dim() == 1:\\n            agent_pos = agent_pos.unsqueeze(0)\\n        return_observations[OBS_STATE] = agent_pos\\n\\n    if \\\"robot_state\\\" in observations:\\n        return_observations[f\\\"{OBS_STR}.robot_state\\\"] = _convert_nested_dict(observations[\\\"robot_state\\\"])\\n\\n    # Handle IsaacLab Arena format: observations have 'policy' and 'camera_obs' keys\\n    if \\\"policy\\\" in observations:\\n        return_observations[f\\\"{OBS_STR}.policy\\\"] = observations[\\\"policy\\\"]\\n\\n    if \\\"camera_obs\\\" in observations:\\n        return_observations[f\\\"{OBS_STR}.camera_obs\\\"] = observations[\\\"camera_obs\\\"]\\n\\n    return return_observations\\n\\n\\ndef env_to_policy_features(env_cfg: EnvConfig) -> dict[str, PolicyFeature]:\\n    # TODO(jadechoghari, imstevenpmwork): remove this hardcoding of keys and just use the nested keys as is\\n    # (need to also refactor preprocess_observation and externalize normalization from policies)\\n    policy_features = {}\\n    for key, ft in env_cfg.features.items():\\n        if ft.type is FeatureType.VISUAL:\\n            if len(ft.shape) != 3:\\n                raise ValueError(f\\\"Number of dimensions of {key} != 3 (shape={ft.shape})\\\")\\n\\n            shape = get_channel_first_image_shape(ft.shape)\\n            feature = PolicyFeature(type=ft.type, shape=shape)\\n        else:\\n            feature = ft\\n\\n        policy_key = env_cfg.features_map[key]\\n        policy_features[policy_key] = feature\\n\\n    return policy_features\\n\\n\\ndef _sub_env_has_attr(env: gym.vector.VectorEnv, attr: str) -> bool:\\n    try:\\n        env.get_attr(attr)\\n        return True\\n    except (AttributeError, Exception):\\n        return False\\n\\n\\nclass _LazyAsyncVectorEnv:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Defers AsyncVectorEnv creation until first use.\\n\\n    Creating a\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 4682 字符] ...\\n\\nath\\n\\n\\ndef _download_hub_file(\\n    cfg_str: str,\\n    trust_remote_code: bool,\\n    hub_cache_dir: str | None,\\n) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Parse `cfg_str` (hub URL), enforce `trust_remote_code`, and return\\n    (repo_id, file_path, local_file, revision).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not trust_remote_code:\\n        raise RuntimeError(\\n            f\\\"Refusing to execute remote code from the Hub for '{cfg_str}'. \\\"\\n            \\\"Executing hub env modules runs arbitrary Python code from third-party repositories. \\\"\\n            \\\"If you trust this repo and understand the risks, call `make_env(..., trust_remote_code=True)` \\\"\\n            \\\"and prefer pinning to a specific revision: 'user/repo@&lt;commit-hash>:env.py'.\\\"\\n        )\\n\\n    repo_id, revision, file_path = _parse_hub_url(cfg_str)\\n\\n    try:\\n        local_file = hf_hub_download(\\n            repo_id=repo_id, filename=file_path, revision=revision, cache_dir=hub_cache_dir\\n        )\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        # fallback to snapshot download\\n        snapshot_dir = snapshot_download(repo_id=repo_id, revision=revision, cache_dir=hub_cache_dir)\\n        local_file = os.path.join(snapshot_dir, file_path)\\n        if not os.path.exists(local_file):\\n            raise FileNotFoundError(\\n                f\\\"Could not find {file_path} in repository {repo_id}@{revision or 'main'}\\\"\\n            ) from e\\n\\n    return repo_id, file_path, local_file, revision\\n\\n\\ndef _import_hub_module(local_file: str, repo_id: str) -> Any:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Import the downloaded file as a module and surface helpful import error messages.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    module_name = f\\\"hub_env_{repo_id.replace('/', '_')}\\\"\\n    try:\\n        module = _load_module_from_path(local_file, module_name=module_name)\\n    except ModuleNotFoundError as e:\\n        missing = getattr(e, \\\"name\\\", None) or str(e)\\n        raise ModuleNotFoundError(\\n            f\\\"Hub env '{repo_id}:{os.path.basename(local_file)}' failed to import because the dependency \\\"\\n            f\\\"'{missing}' is not installed locally.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n        ) from e\\n    except ImportError as e:\\n        raise ImportError(\\n            f\\\"Failed to load hub env module '{repo_id}:{os.path.basename(local_file)}'. Import error: {e}\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n        ) from e\\n    return module\\n\\n\\ndef _call_make_env(module: Any, n_envs: int, use_async_envs: bool, cfg: EnvConfig | None) -> Any:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Ensure module exposes make_env and call it.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not hasattr(module, \\\"make_env\\\"):\\n        raise AttributeError(\\n            f\\\"The hub module {getattr(module, '__name__', 'hub_module')} must expose `make_env(n_envs=int, use_async_envs=bool)`.\\\"\\n        )\\n    entry_fn = module.make_env\\n    # Only pass cfg if it's not None (i.e., when an EnvConfig was provided, not a string hub ID)\\n    if cfg is not None:\\n        return entry_fn(n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs, cfg=cfg)\\n    else:\\n        return entry_fn(n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs)\\n\\n\\ndef _normalize_hub_result(result: Any) -> dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Normalize possible return types from hub `make_env` into the mapping:\\n      { suite_name: { task_id: vector_env } }\\n    Accepts:\\n      - dict (assumed already correct)\\n      - gym.vector.VectorEnv\\n      - gym.Env (will be wrapped into SyncVectorEnv)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if isinstance(result, dict):\\n        return result\\n\\n    # VectorEnv: use its spec.id if available\\n    if isinstance(result, gym.vector.VectorEnv):\\n        suite_name = getattr(result, \\\"spec\\\", None) and getattr(result.spec, \\\"id\\\", None) or \\\"hub_env\\\"\\n        return {suite_name: {0: result}}\\n\\n    # Single Env: wrap into SyncVectorEnv\\n    if isinstance(result, gym.Env):\\n        vec = gym.vector.SyncVectorEnv([lambda: result])\\n        suite_name = getattr(result, \\\"spec\\\", None) and getattr(result.spec, \\\"id\\\", None) or \\\"hub_env\\\"\\n        return {suite_name: {0: vec}}\\n\\n    raise ValueError(\\n        \\\"Hub `make_env` must return either a mapping {suite: {task_id: vec_env}}, \\\"\\n        \\\"a gym.vector.VectorEnv, or a single gym.Env.\\\"\\n    )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.42}}"}}}

> /tmp/official_eval_test.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Test SmolVLA model using the FULL official lerobot pipeline.\nUses LiberoEnv + env_preprocessor + policy preprocessor + select_action + postprocessor.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nos.environ[\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\nos.environ[\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n\nimport sys, time, json\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# lerobot imports\nfrom lerobot.policies.smolvla.modeling_smolvla import SmolVLAPolicy\nfrom lerobot.policies.smolvla.processor_smolvla import make_smolvla_pre_post_processors\nfrom lerobot.processor.env_processor import LiberoProcessorStep\nfrom lerobot.processor.pipeline import PolicyProcessorPipeline\nfrom lerobot.envs.utils import preprocess_observation\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\n\nPOLICY_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero\"\n\nprint(\"Loading policy...\", flush=True)\npolicy = SmolVLAPolicy.from_pretrained(POLICY_PATH)\npolicy.to(\"cuda\")\npolicy.eval()\nfor p in policy.parameters():\n    p.requires_grad = False\n\n# Build preprocessor + postprocessor from saved stats\nstats_file = Path(POLICY_PATH) / \"policy_preprocessor_step_5_normalizer_processor.safetensors\"\npost_stats_file = Path(POLICY_PATH) / \"policy_postprocessor_step_0_unnormalizer_processor.safetensors\"\npre_stats = load_file(str(stats_file))\npost_stats = load_file(str(post_stats_file))\n\n# Build stats dict for processors\ndataset_stats = {}\nfor k, v in pre_stats.items():\n    parts = k.rsplit(\".\", 1)\n    if len(parts) == 2:\n        feat, stat = parts\n        if feat not in dataset_stats:\n            dataset_stats[feat] = {}\n        dataset_stats[feat][stat] = v\nfor k, v in post_stats.items():\n    parts = k.rsplit(\".\", 1)\n    if len(parts) == 2:\n        feat, stat = parts\n        if feat not in dataset_stats:\n            dataset_stats[feat] = {}\n        dataset_stats[feat][stat] = v\n\npreprocessor, postprocessor = make_smolvla_pre_post_processors(policy.config, dataset_stats=dataset_stats)\n\n# Environment processors\nenv_preprocessor = PolicyProcessorPipeline(steps=[LiberoProcessorStep()])\nenv_postprocessor = PolicyProcessorPipeline(steps=[])\n\nprint(\"Loading LIBERO object envs...\", flush=True)\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark as bm\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\nbenchmark_instance = bm.get_benchmark(\"libero_object\")()\nn_tasks = 2  # test first 2 tasks\nn_episodes = 2\nmax_steps = 280\n\nresults = []\nfor task_idx in range(n_tasks):\n    task = benchmark_instance.get_task(task_idx)\n    task_bddl = benchmark_instance.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_idx)\n    init_states = benchmark_instance.get_task_init_states(task_idx)\n    \n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(\n        bddl_file_name=task_bddl,\n        camera_heights=256,\n        camera_widths=256,\n        camera_names=[\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n    )\n    env.seed(42)\n    \n    # Get task description - use same format as LiberoEnv\n    task_desc = task.language\n    print(f\"\\nTask {task_idx}: {task_desc}\", flush=True)\n\n    # Build camera name mapping (same as default LiberoEnv)\n    camera_name_mapping = {\n        \"agentview_image\": \"camera1\",\n        \"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\": \"camera2\",\n    }\n\n    for ep_idx in range(n_episodes):\n        env.reset()\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep_idx])\n        policy.reset()\n\n        t0 = time.time()\n        for step in range(max_steps):\n            # Build raw gym-style observation (mimic LiberoEnv._format_raw_obs)\n            images = {}\n            for cam in [\"agentview_image\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]:\n                images[camera_name_mapping[cam]] = obs[cam]\n            \n            eef_pos = obs.get(\"robot0_eef_pos\")\n            eef_quat = obs.get(\"robot0_eef_quat\")\n            eef_mat = env.robots[0].controller.ee_ori_mat\n            gripper_qpos = obs.get(\"robot0_gripper_qpos\")\n            gripper_qvel = obs.get(\"robot0_gripper_qvel\")\n            joint_pos = obs.get(\"robot0_joint_pos\")\n            joint_vel = obs.get(\"robot0_joint_vel\")\n            \n            raw_obs = {\n                \"pixels\": images,\n                \"robot_state\": {\n                    \"eef\": {\"pos\": eef_pos, \"quat\": eef_quat, \"mat\": eef_mat},\n                    \"gripper\": {\"qpos\": gripper_qpos, \"qvel\": gripper_qvel},\n                    \"joints\": {\"pos\": joint_pos, \"vel\": joint_vel},\n                },\n            }\n\n            # Step 1: preprocess_observation (numpy -> tensor, rename keys)\n            processed_obs = preprocess_observation(raw_obs)\n            \n            # Step 2: Add task description\n            processed_obs[\"task\"] = [task_desc]\n            \n            # Step 3: env_preprocessor (LiberoProcessorStep - flip images, flatten state)\n            processed_obs = env_preprocessor(processed_obs)\n            \n            # Step 4: policy preprocessor (rename, batch, newline, tokenize, device, normalize)\n            processed_obs = preprocessor(processed_obs)\n            \n            # Step 5: select_action (returns normalized action)\n            with torch.inference_mode():\n                action = policy.select_action(processed_obs)\n            \n            # Step 6: postprocessor (unnormalize action)\n            action = postprocessor(action)\n            \n            # Step 7: to numpy\n            action_np = action.to(\"cpu\").numpy().squeeze()\n            \n            # Step 8: env step\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action_np)\n            \n            success = env.check_success()\n            if success:\n                break\n        \n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        result = {\n            \"task\": task_desc,\n            \"episode\": ep_idx,\n            \"success\": bool(success),\n            \"steps\": step + 1,\n            \"time\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        }\n        results.append(result)\n        print(json.dumps(result), flush=True)\n    \n    env.close()\n\nn_success = sum(r[\"success\"] for r in results)\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\nprint(f\"Results: {n_success}/{len(results)} success ({100*n_success/len(results):.0f}%)\", flush=True)\nPYEOF\necho \"Script {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01S3eewcX2EvMrpXpvyBYejj","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"def _convert_nested_dict(d):\\\\n    result = {}\\\\n    for k, v in d.items():\\\\n        if isinstance(v, dict):\\\\n            result[k] = _convert_nested_dict(v)\\\\n        elif isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\\\\n            result[k] = torch.from_numpy(v)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            result[k] = v\\\\n    return result\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef preprocess_observation(observations: dict[str, np.ndarray]) -> dict[str, Tensor]:\\\\n    # TODO(jadechoghari, imstevenpmwork): refactor this to use features from the environment (no hardcoding)\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Convert environment observation to LeRobot format observation.\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        observation: Dictionary of observation batches from a Gym vector environment.\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        Dictionary of observation batches with keys renamed to LeRobot format and values as tensors.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    # map to expected inputs for the policy\\\\n    return_observations = {}\\\\n    if \\\\\\\"pixels\\\\\\\" in observations:\\\\n        if isinstance(observations[\\\\\\\"pixels\\\\\\\"], dict):\\\\n            imgs = {f\\\\\\\"{OBS_IMAGES}.{key}\\\\\\\": img for key, img in observations[\\\\\\\"pixels\\\\\\\"].items()}\\\\n        else:\\\\n            imgs = {OBS_IMAGE: observations[\\\\\\\"pixels\\\\\\\"]}\\\\n\\\\n        for imgkey, img in imgs.items():\\\\n            # TODO(aliberts, rcadene): use transforms.ToTensor()?\\\\n            img_tensor = torch.from_numpy(img)\\\\n\\\\n            # When preprocessing observations in a non-vectorized environment, we need to add a batch dimension.\\\\n            # This is the case for human-in-the-loop RL where there is only one environment.\\\\n            if img_tensor.ndim == 3:\\\\n                img_tensor = img_tensor.unsqueeze(0)\\\\n            # sanity check that images are channel last\\\\n            _, h, w, c = img_tensor.shape\\\\n            assert c < h and c < w, f\\\\\\\"expect channel last images, but instead got {img_tensor.shape=}\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n            # sanity check that images are uint8\\\\n            assert img_tensor.dtype == torch.uint8, f\\\\\\\"expect torch.uint8, but instead {img_tensor.dtype=}\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n            # convert to channel first of type float32 in range [0,1]\\\\n            img_tensor = einops.rearrange(img_tensor, \\\\\\\"b h w c -> b c h w\\\\\\\").contiguous()\\\\n            img_tensor = img_tensor.type(torch.float32)\\\\n            img_tensor /= 255\\\\n\\\\n            return_observations[imgkey] = img_tensor\\\\n\\\\n    if \\\\\\\"environment_state\\\\\\\" in observations:\\\\n        env_state = torch.from_numpy(observations[\\\\\\\"environment_state\\\\\\\"]).float()\\\\n        if env_state.dim() == 1:\\\\n            env_state = env_state.unsqueeze(0)\\\\n\\\\n        return_observations[OBS_ENV_STATE] = env_state\\\\n\\\\n    if \\\\\\\"agent_pos\\\\\\\" in observations:\\\\n        agent_pos = torch.from_numpy(observations[\\\\\\\"agent_pos\\\\\\\"]).float()\\\\n        if agent_pos.dim() == 1:\\\\n            agent_pos = agent_pos.unsqueeze(0)\\\\n        return_observations[OBS_STATE] = agent_pos\\\\n\\\\n    if \\\\\\\"robot_state\\\\\\\" in observations:\\\\n        return_observations[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_STR}.robot_state\\\\\\\"] = _convert_nested_dict(observations[\\\\\\\"robot_state\\\\\\\"])\\\\n\\\\n    #
